5.2.1.
Case (a) : (C, C, C)

First, let us consider the non-pairing cases, where
k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{2}^{\prime}
and
k_{2}^{\prime}\neq k_{3}^{\prime}

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k^{\prime}_{2},k_{3}^{\prime}\\
k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{2}^{\prime},k_{2}^{\prime}\neq k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}

(5.13)

where
h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}=h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}(\omega)
satisfies
(
5.11
) and
{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}
is defined in
(
2.44
).

Applying Proposition
2.24
, Remark
2.25
, and Proposition
2.17
yields
7
7

7

Here we only consider the case
N_{\min}=N_{\max}
for simplicity. When
N_{\min}<N_{\max}
, we need to apply Proposition
2.24
and Proposition
2.17
repeatedly as in Subsection

, as where

.

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\alpha/2}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\bigg\|_{kk_{1}^{\prime}k_{2}^{\prime}k_{3}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\alpha/2}\big\|\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(5.14)

Then from (
5.11
) and Lemma
2.12
, we can continue with

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\alpha/2}\min\big(N_{3}^{2-\alpha}\log(N_{1}\vee N_{2})(N_{1}\wedge N_{2})+N_{1}N_{2},\\
&\hskip 42.67912ptN_{1}^{2-\alpha}\log(N_{2}\vee N_{3})(N_{2}\wedge N_{3})+N_{2}N_{3}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\alpha/2}N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\log N_{\max})^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}N_{\med}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},\end{split}

(5.15)

which is sufficient for (
5.9
) since
N_{\med}\gtrsim N_{\max}^{1-\delta}
, provided
\varepsilon,\delta,\gamma\ll\alpha-1
.

Secondly, we will consider the case when
k_{1}^{\prime}=k_{2}^{\prime}
(the case when
k_{2}^{\prime}=k_{3}^{\prime}
is similar, and we omit its proof), where we have
N_{1}=N_{2}
.
For this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{k}&=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\\
&=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{(|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}-1)g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt+\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\\
&=\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(1)}+\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(2)}.\end{split}

(5.16)

For the term
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(1)}
, we can apply Proposition
2.24
with
\eta_{k_{1}^{\prime}}=(|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}-1)
,
\eta_{k_{3}^{\prime}}=g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}
, or
\eta_{k_{1}^{\prime}}=|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}
for over paired cases, and then similar argument as above, to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(1)}\|_{k}&\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\bigg\|_{kk_{1}^{\prime}k_{3}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}\big\|_{k_{1}k_{2}\to k_{1}^{\prime}}\big\|h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\big\|_{k_{3}\to k_{3}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}},\end{split}

(5.17)

where we used Lemma
2.18
in the last step. By using the same estimate as in (
5.15
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(1)}\|_{k}\lesssim N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}N_{\med}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},

(5.18)

which is again sufficient for (
5.9
).
Now we turn to the second term in (
5.16
).
By using the unitary property of
\widetilde{H}_{kk^{\prime}}^{N,L_{N}}
in Lemma
3.6
(also see Corollary
3.7
and Remark
3.8
), we may redefine
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(2)}
as follows, still denoted by
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(2)}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(2)}&=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\bigg(\frac{1}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}}-\frac{1}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}}\bigg)h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}}\\
&=:\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(21)}+\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(22)}.\end{split}

(5.19)

Recall that the support of
h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}
is in
\{|k_{1}-k_{1}^{\prime}|\lesssim N_{1}^{\varepsilon}L_{1}\}
. Therefore,

\left|\frac{1}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}-\frac{1}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}\right|\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}.

(5.20)

Let us consider
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(21)}
and
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(22)}

one-by-one.
Let us first consider
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(21)}
first. From (
5.19
) and (
5.20
), we apply Proposition
2.24
and
Proposition
2.17
to get

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(21)}\|_{k}&\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\bigg\|_{kk_{3}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\big\|_{k_{1}k_{1}^{\prime}}\prod_{j=2}^{3}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}},\end{split}

(5.21)

which together with Lemma
2.12
and (
5.11
) implies that

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(21)}\|_{k}\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}N_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}L_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha+\varepsilon+\gamma_{0}}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}N_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.22)

which is sufficient for (
5.9
) again since
N_{1},N_{3}\gtrsim N_{\max}^{1-\delta}
.
For the second term of (
5.16
), by applying Proposition
2.24
and Proposition
2.17
similarly as above, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(22)}\|_{k}&\lesssim\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}}\bigg\|_{k}\\
&\lesssim N_{\max}^{-\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\bigg\|_{kk_{1}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim N_{\max}^{-\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}},\end{split}

(5.23)

where we used Lemma
2.18
twice in the last step.
By using Lemma
2.12
and (
5.11
), from (
5.23
) we get

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(22)}\|_{k}\lesssim N_{\max}^{-\frac{3\alpha}{2}}N_{\max}^{1+\varepsilon},

(5.24)

where we used
N_{1}=N_{2}=N_{3}=N_{\max}
, which is again sufficient for (
5.9
).

Finally,
by collecting (
5.16
), (
5.18
), (
5.19
), (
5.22
), and (
5.24
), we conclude that

\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim N_{\max}^{-\frac{1}{2}-4\gamma}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}-4\gamma}.

Thus we finish the proof of (
5.9
).

5.2.2.
Case (b) : (C, C, D)

First, consider the non-pairing case when
k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{2}^{\prime}
. In this case, we have

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{2}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}},

where
h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}=h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}(\omega)
satisfies
(
5.11
),
(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}
satisfies (
5.12
) and

{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}
is defined in (
2.44
).
From Proposition
2.17
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{2}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\bigg\|_{k\to k_{3}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}.

(5.25)

Then we apply Proposition
2.24
and Proposition
2.17
repeatedly as in Subsection
4.2.1
to the right-hand side of (
5.25
) to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big(\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}\to k_{3}}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{k\to k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\\
&\hphantom{XXXXXXXX}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}\to k_{2}k_{3}}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{2}\to k_{1}k_{3}}\Big)\\
&\hphantom{XXX}\times\prod_{j=1}^{2}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}.\end{split}

(5.26)

Then by using Lemma
2.14
and (
5.11
), from (
5.26
) we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}\big(N_{1}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{2}^{\varepsilon}+N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+(N_{1}\wedge N_{2})^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{3}^{\varepsilon}+N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{XX}+(N_{2}\wedge N_{3})^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{1}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+(N_{1}\wedge N_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{4}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{4}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}N_{\min}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}},

which is sufficient for our purpose since
N_{\med}\gtrsim N_{\max}^{1-\delta}
and
\varepsilon,\delta,\gamma\ll\alpha-1
.

Let us turn to the pairing case, i.e.
k_{1}^{\prime}=k^{\prime}_{2}
(which implies
N_{1}=N_{2}
).
By using Lemma
3.6
, we may consider

\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{k}&=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{k_{1}^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}-\frac{1}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}\right)h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\\
&\quad+\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{k_{1}^{\prime}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}\frac{(|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}-1)}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\\
&=:\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(3)}+\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(4)}.\end{split}

Similar argument as in (
5.21
) gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(3)}\|_{k}&\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{k_{1}^{\prime}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\bigg\|_{k}\\
&\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}\to k_{3}}\big\|h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\big\|_{k_{1}k_{1}^{\prime}}\big\|h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}\big\|_{k_{2}\to k_{2}^{\prime}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}\\
&\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}L_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}\\
&\lesssim N^{-\alpha+\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon+\gamma_{0}}_{1}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma},\end{split}

where we used
N_{1}=N_{2}
, which is sufficient for our purpose since
N_{1},N_{3}\gtrsim N_{\max}^{1-\delta}
.
For the term
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(4)}
, by Proposition
2.24
with
\eta_{k_{1}^{\prime}}=|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}-1
, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(4)}\|_{k}&=\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{k_{1}^{\prime}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}\frac{(|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}-1)}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\bigg\|_{k}\\
&\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha}\bigg(\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}\bigg\|_{kk_{1}^{\prime}\rightarrow k_{3}}\\
&\hphantom{XXXX}+\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}\bigg\|_{k\rightarrow k_{1}^{\prime}k_{3}}\bigg)\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}.\end{split}

Then similar argument as in (
5.26
) together with Lemma
2.14
and Lemma
2.18
yields

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(4)}\|_{k}\lesssim&N_{1}^{-\alpha}\big(\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}\to k_{3}}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{k\to k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big)\\
&\times\big\|h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{2}}}\big\|_{k_{1}^{\prime}\to k_{1}k_{2}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}\\
\lesssim&N_{1}^{-\alpha+\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma},\end{split}

which is again sufficient for our purpose.

The proof for Case (d): (D, C, C) is similar to that of Case (b) (C, C, D); we omit the proof.

5.2.3.
Case (c) : (C, D, C)

In this case, there is no pairing. Therefore, we have

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\overline{(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}{h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}{g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}},

where
h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}=h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}(\omega)
satisfies
(
5.11
),
(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}
satisfies (
5.12
) and

{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}
is defined in (
2.44
).
Applying Proposition
2.24
and Propostion
2.17
similarly as in the previous subsection, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\Big(\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{3}\to k_{2}}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}\to k_{2}k_{3}}\\
&\hphantom{XXXXXXX}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{3}\to k_{1}k_{2}}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{k\to k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\Big)\\
&\hphantom{XXX}\times\big\|h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\big\|_{k_{1}^{\prime}\to k_{1}}\big\|{h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}}\big\|_{k_{3}^{\prime}\to k_{3}}\|(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}\|_{k_{2}}.\end{split}

Then by using Lemma
2.14
, (
5.11
), and (
5.12
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim(N_{1}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}N_{\min}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}},

which is sufficient for our purpose since
N_{\med}\gtrsim N_{\max}^{1-\delta}
and
\varepsilon,\gamma,\delta\ll\alpha-1
.

5.2.4.
Case (e) : (D, D, C)

In this case, we have

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\overline{(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}}\sum_{k_{3}^{\prime}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}},

where
h^{N_{3},L_{3}}_{k_{3}k_{3}^{\prime}}
satisfies
(
5.11
),
(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}
and
\overline{(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}}
satisfy (
5.12
), and the base tensor

{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}
is defined in (
2.44
).
From Proposition
2.24
and Propostion
2.17
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big(\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{3}\to k_{1}k_{2}}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{k\to k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big)\\
&\hphantom{XXX}\times\big\|{h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}}\big\|_{k_{3}^{\prime}\to k_{3}}\|(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\|_{k_{1}}\|(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}\|_{k_{2}}.\end{split}

Then by using Lemma
2.13
and (
5.11
)-(
5.12
) to get

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}\big((N_{\min}N_{\max})^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+(N_{1}\wedge N_{2})^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(\log N_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}}+N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big),

which is again sufficient for our purpose since
N_{\med}\gtrsim N_{\max}^{1-\delta}
.

The proof for Case (f): (C, D, D) is similar to that of Case (e): (D, D, C); we omit the proof.

5.2.5.
Case (g) : (D, C, D)

In this case, we have

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}},

where
h^{N_{2},L_{2}}_{k_{2}k_{2}^{\prime}}=h^{N_{2},L_{2}}_{k_{2}k_{2}^{\prime}}(\omega)
satisfies
(
5.11
),
(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}
and
\left({w_{N_{3}}}\right)_{k_{3}}
satisfy (
5.12
), and the base tensor

{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}
is defined in (
2.44
).
From Proposition
2.24
and Proposition
2.17
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big(\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}\to k_{3}}+\big\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|_{kk_{1}\to k_{2}k_{3}}\big)\\
&\hphantom{XXX}\times\big\|h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}\big\|_{k_{2}^{\prime}\to k_{2}}\|(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\|_{k_{1}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}.\end{split}

(5.27)

Then by using Lemma
2.13
, Lemma
2.14
, (
5.11
), and (
5.12
) to get

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(N_{1}N_{3})^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}\big(N_{1}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(\log N_{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}+N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+(N_{\min}N_{\max})^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big),

which is again sufficient for our purpose since
N_{\med}\gtrsim N_{\max}^{1-\delta}
.

5.2.6.
Case (h) : (D, D, D)

In this case, we have

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\overline{(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}},

where
(w_{N_{j}})_{k_{j}}
for
j\in\{1,2,3\}
satisfy (
5.12
),
and the base tensor
{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}
is defined in (
2.44
).
By applying Proposition
2.17
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim\min\big(\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\|_{kk_{3}\to k_{1}k_{2}},\|{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\|_{kk_{1}\to k_{2}k_{3}}\big)\prod_{j=1}^{3}\|(w_{N_{j}})_{k_{j}}\|_{k_{j}}.\end{split}

By using Lemma
2.13
and (
5.12
), we get

\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}(N_{\min}N_{\med})^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}},

which is sufficient for (
5.12
).

5.3.
random averaging operator

In this section, we estimate terms (II) and (IV), which correspond to

\mathcal{P}^{+}
and
\mathcal{P}^{-}
of (
3.6
), respectively. We only consider the term (II) since the argument for the term (IV) is similar.
To show (
5.4
) for the term (II), it
suffices to show

\|\mathcal{P}^{+}[z_{M}]\|_{X^{b}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}.

(5.28)

We see from the definition of terms (II) that
N_{\med}=\max(N_{1},N_{2})
since both
N_{1}
and
N_{2}
are smaller than
N_{3}
and
N_{3}
is approximately equal to
M
. The case where
N_{\med}\gtrsim M^{1-\delta}
corresponds to the high-high interaction, and we have already established (
5.28
) for this case in the previous subsection. Therefore, we will only concentrate on the case where
N_{\med}\ll M^{1-\delta}
in the following discussion. Applying (
4.15
) and (
5.12
), we obtain

\|\mathcal{P}^{\pm}[z_{M}]\|_{X^{b}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{P}^{\pm}\|_{Y^{b,b}}\|z_{M}\|_{X^{b}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}.

This completes the proof of (
5.4
) for this case.

5.4.
The Term (V)

This subsection shows that the term (V) satisfies the bound (
5.6
). It suffices to show (
5.9
). We
may assume that
N_{1},N_{3}\ll N_{2}^{1-\delta}
; otherwise, it has been dealt with in Subsection
5.2
.
As
w_{N_{2}}
is of type (C), we only need to consider the cases where the types of
(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})
are
(a) (C, C, C), (b) (C, C, D), (d) (D, C, C), (g) (D, C, D). We set
N_{2}=M

in this subsection.

Before proceeding, let us recall that

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathrm{T}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}^{\text{b},m}\cdot(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\cdot\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k_{2}^{\prime}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{\overline{g_{k_{2}^{\prime}}(\omega)}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha/2}}\cdot(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}

(5.29)

where we may take that
N_{1},N_{3}\ll N_{2}^{1-\delta}
with
h_{k_{2}k_{2}^{\prime}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}(\omega)
supported in the set
\{(k_{2},k_{2}^{\prime});\frac{N_{2}}{2}<\langle k_{2}^{\prime}\rangle\leq N_{2},\,|k_{2}-k_{2}^{\prime}|\lesssim N_{2}^{\varepsilon}L_{2}\}
,

\mathcal{B}_{\leq L_{2}}
-measurable for some
L_{2}\leq N_{2}^{1-\delta}
, and satisfying the bounds (
5.11
).
We note that
|k_{2}-k_{2}^{\prime}|\lesssim N_{2}^{\varepsilon}L_{2}\lesssim N_{2}^{\delta/2}N^{1-\delta}_{2}
, which together with
\langle k_{2}^{\prime}\rangle\geq N_{2}/2
implies that
|k_{2}|\geq{N_{2}}/{4}
, provided
N_{2}
is sufficiently large. Furthermore, from
N_{1},N_{3}\ll N_{2}^{1-\delta}
, we have

|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|.

(5.30)

From (
2.14
) and (
5.30
), we have that

|2k-(k_{1}+k_{3})|=|2k_{2}-(k_{1}+k_{3})|\gtrsim|k_{1}+k_{3}|,

which, together with (
2.27
), implies that the summand of (
5.29
) is non-zero only for those
(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})
such that

(k,k_{2})\in S_{k_{1}k_{3}}^{\rm good},

(5.31)

where
S_{k_{1}k_{3}}^{\rm good}
is given in (
2.27
).
The above observation will be crucial in our later analysis. In particular, instead of (
5.29
), we will consider the following

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}\\
|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|\end{subarray}}\mathrm{T}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}^{\text{b},m}\cdot(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\cdot\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k_{2}^{\prime}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{\overline{g_{k_{2}^{\prime}}(\omega)}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha/2}}\cdot(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}.

(5.32)

Now, let us consider (
5.32
) with the restriction (
5.30
) (or (
5.31
)) case-by-case.

5.4.1.
Case (a) : (C, C, C)

There is no pairing since
N_{1},N_{3}\ll N_{2}=M
. So we have

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}\\
|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|\end{subarray}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k^{\prime}_{2},k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}.

Same argument as in Subsection
5.2.1
, followed by Proposition
2.17
and Lemma
2.15
, yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\bigg\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}\\
|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|\end{subarray}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\bigg\|_{kk_{1}^{\prime}k_{2}^{\prime}k_{3}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(N_{1}N_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},\end{split}

which is sufficient for our purpose.

5.4.2.
Case (b) : (C, C, D)

Similar to the above, there is no pairing since
N_{1}\ll N_{2}=M
. From (
5.32
), we have

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}\\
|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{2}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}},

where
h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}
for
j=1,2
satisfy
(
5.11
),
(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}
satisfies (
5.12
), and

{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}
is defined in (
2.44
).
By a similar argument as in (
5.26
) together with (
2.2
),
we have

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim\bigg\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2}\\
|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{2}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\bigg\|_{k\to k_{3}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}\\
&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{|k_{1}+k_{3}|<|k_{2}|}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\prod_{j=1}^{2}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}.\end{split}

Then we apply Lemma
2.15
, together with (
5.11
) and (
5.12
), to get

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}(N_{1}N_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},\end{split}

which is enough for our purpose.

The proof for Case (d): (D, C, C) is similar to that of Case (b): (C, C, D); we omit the proof.

5.4.3.
Case (g) : (D, C, D)

In this case, we consider

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}\\
|k_{1}+k_{3}|\ll|k_{2}|\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{k_{2}^{\prime}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}\frac{\overline{g_{k^{\prime}_{2}}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}.

By a similar argument as in (
5.27
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{|k_{1}+k_{3}|<|k_{2}|}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{2}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}\big\|_{k_{2}^{\prime}\to k_{2}}\|(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\|_{k_{1}}\|(w_{N_{3}})_{k_{3}}\|_{k_{3}}.\end{split}

Then from Lemma
2.15
, (
5.11
), and (
5.12
), we have

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}_{1}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{|k_{1}+k_{3}|<|k_{2}|}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\\
&\lesssim N^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}_{1}N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},\end{split}

which is again sufficient for (
5.9
).

5.5.
The
\Gamma
-condition case

In this subsection, we consider (
5.5
), which shows that the term (VI), defined at the beginning of Section
5
, satisfies the bound

\|\mathscr{B}(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})\|_{X^{b}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma},

(5.33)

where
\mathscr{B}
is given in (
5.3
).
Same argument as in Subsection
5.1
reduces (
5.33
) to the
the following estimate

\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}-3\gamma},

(5.34)

where
\mathscr{X}_{k}
is given in (
5.10
).

To prove (
5.34
), we make several assumptions. To make the term (VI) non-trivial, we should have
N_{\max}\gtrsim M
. If
N_{2}\gtrsim M
, then
term (VI) can be handled in the same way as the terms (IV) and (V). Therefore, we only need to consider the cases that
N_{1}
or
N_{3}\gtrsim M
.
Due to the symmetry between
w_{N_{1}}
and
w_{N_{3}}
, we may assume
N_{3}\gtrsim M
in what follows.
To prove (
5.34
), it only suffices to show

\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim N_{3}^{-\frac{1}{2}-3\gamma},

(5.35)

where
\mathscr{X}_{k}
is given in (
5.10
).
We may further assume that
{{N_{1},N_{2}\ll N_{3}^{1-\delta}}}
as otherwise it has been dealt with in Subsection

5.2
. Furthermore, we may assume that
{{w_{N_{3}}=\psi_{N_{3},L_{N_{3}}}}}
; otherwise, it can be handled by using the random averaging operator as in Subsection
5.3
.

Due to the projections
\Delta_{M}

(
\frac{M}{2}<\langle k\rangle\leq M
), the requirement
N_{\max}\leq\frac{M}{2}
in the definition of the term (VI), we observe that

\begin{split}\langle k\rangle>\frac{M}{2}\geq\langle k_{3}\rangle&\Longrightarrow|k|\geq\Big(\Big(\frac{M}{2}\Big)^{2}-1\Big)^{1/2}\geq|k_{3}|=|k-k_{1}+k_{2}|\geq|k|-2N_{\med}\\
&\Longrightarrow|k_{3}|+2N_{\med}\geq|k|\geq\Gamma\geq|k_{3}|,\end{split}

where
\Gamma:=(({M}/{2})^{2}-1)^{1/2}
.
Therefore, to make
\mathscr{X}_{k}
is non-trivial, we need
(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})\in B_{\Gamma}
, where
B_{\Gamma}
is given in (
2.37
).

With the above argument, we focus on (
5.35
) with

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})\mathrm{T}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}^{\text{b},m}\cdot(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\cdot\overline{(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}},

(5.36)

where
N_{1},N_{2}\ll N_{3}
.
In the following, we distinguish several cases for the possible types of
(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}})
: Case (a) (C, C), Case (c)
(C, D), Case (d) (D, C), Case (e) (D, D).

5.5.1.
Case (a) : (C, C, C)

We start with the non-pairing cases.
Same argument as in Subsection
5.2.1
, followed by Corollary
2.16
, yields

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}\mathrm{T}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}^{\text{b},m}\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2}M)^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\min}N^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\med}\lesssim N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},\end{split}

which is sufficient for (
5.35
).

We turn to the pairing cases.
Similar as in Subsection
5.2.1
, we can write (
5.36
) (note that
w_{N_{1}}
and
w_{N_{2}}
are both of type (C)) as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{k}&=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\\
&=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{(|g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}|^{2}-1)g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt+\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime}\neq k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\overline{h_{k_{2}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{2},L_{2}}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{{\alpha}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\\
&=\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(1)}+\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(2)}.\end{split}

For the first term
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(1)}
, we follow the computation in (
5.17
) and use Corollary
2.16
to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(1)}\|_{k}&\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}\mathrm{T}^{\text{b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha+1}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},\end{split}

which is enough for our purpose. As to the second term
\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(2)}
, we follow the computation in (
5.19
), (
5.21
), and (
5.23
) to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}^{(2)}\|_{k}&\lesssim N^{-\alpha-1+\varepsilon}_{1}L_{1}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\big\|_{k_{1}k_{1}^{\prime}}\prod_{j=2}^{3}\big\|h_{k_{j}k^{\prime}_{j}}^{N_{j},L_{j}}\big\|_{k_{j}\to k_{j}^{\prime}}\\
&\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha+\varepsilon+\gamma_{0}}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{\med}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

which is again sufficient since
N_{\min}=N_{\med}=N_{1}
.

5.5.2.
Case (b) : (C, D, C) and Case (c) : (D, C, C)

We consider Case (b) only as the argument for Case (c) is
similar. In this case, we can write (
5.36
) as

\mathscr{X}_{k}=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\cdot\overline{(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}\frac{g_{k^{\prime}_{1}}g_{k^{\prime}_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k^{\prime}_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}.

By a similar argument as in Subsection
5.2.2
,
we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|h_{k_{1}k^{\prime}_{1}}^{N_{1},L_{1}}\big\|_{k_{1}^{\prime}\to k_{1}}\big\|{h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}}\big\|_{k_{3}^{\prime}\to k_{3}}\|(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}\|_{k_{2}}.\end{split}

Then by using Corollary
2.16
, (
5.11
), and (
5.12
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}\lesssim(N_{1}N_{3})^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}N_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}(N_{\min}N_{\med})^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},

which is sufficient for our purpose.

5.5.3.
Case (d) : (D, D, C)

Similar argument as in Subsection
5.2.4
, from (
5.36
) we have

\begin{split}\|\mathscr{X}_{k}\|_{k}&\lesssim N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\big\|{h_{k_{3}k^{\prime}_{3}}^{N_{3},L_{3}}}\big\|_{k_{3}^{\prime}\to k_{3}}\|(w_{N_{1}})_{k_{1}}\|_{k_{1}}\|(w_{N_{2}})_{k_{2}}\|_{k_{2}}\\
&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\big\|\mathbf{1}_{B_{\Gamma}}{\rm T}^{{\rm b},m}_{kk_{1}k_{3}k_{2}}\big\|_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\\
&\lesssim(N_{1}N_{2})^{-\frac{1}{2}-\gamma}N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(N_{\min}N_{\med})^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq N_{3}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}},\end{split}

where we applied Proposition
2.17
in the second step,
which is again sufficient for (
5.34
).

5.6.
Resonant case

Finally, we estimate term (VII), the resonant case.
In this case, we may assume
M=N_{1}=N_{2}=N_{3}
. If
(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})
is of type (D, D, D),
then we have

\begin{split}\|\chi(t)(\operatorname{VII})\|_{X^{b}}&\lesssim\|\mathcal{R}(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})(t)\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\\
&=\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{k}|(w_{N_{1}})_{k}\overline{(w_{N_{2}})_{k}}(w_{N_{3}})_{k}|^{2}dt\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\prod_{i=1}^{3}\|w_{N_{i}}\|_{X^{\frac{1}{3}}}\lesssim\prod_{i=1}^{3}\|w_{N_{i}}\|_{X^{b}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{3}{2}-3\gamma},\end{split}

where we used (
3.18
),
which is sufficient for (
3.5
).

If
(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})
is of type (C, C, C), i.e. Case (a), we have

\|\chi(t)(\operatorname{VII})\|_{X^{b}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{R}(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})(t)\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}},

where
w_{N}
is given by (
3.17
).
We only consider the non-pairing cases, i.e.

\begin{split}\big(&\mathcal{R}(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})(t)\big)_{k}\\
&=\chi(t)\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}{\rm T}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}^{{\rm b},m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}^{\prime},k_{2}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\\
k_{2}^{\prime}\notin\{k_{1}^{\prime},k_{3}^{\prime}\}\end{subarray}}{h^{N_{1},L_{1}}_{kk_{1}^{\prime}}}(t)\overline{{h^{N_{2},L_{2}}_{kk_{2}^{\prime}}}}(t){h^{N_{3},L_{3}}_{kk_{3}^{\prime}}}(t)\frac{g_{k_{1}^{\prime}}\overline{g_{k_{2}^{\prime}}}g_{k_{3}^{\prime}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}^{\prime}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}.\end{split}

Then, from Proposition
2.24
and then Proposition
2.17
, we have

\begin{split}\|&\mathcal{R}(w_{N_{1}},w_{N_{2}},w_{N_{3}})(t)\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}}\\
&\lesssim M^{-\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\big\|\chi(t){h^{N_{1},L_{1}}_{kk_{1}^{\prime}}}\overline{{h^{N_{2},L_{2}}_{kk_{2}^{\prime}}}}{h^{N_{3},L_{3}}_{kk_{3}^{\prime}}}\big\|_{L^{2}_{T}(kk_{1}^{\prime}k_{2}^{\prime}k_{3}^{\prime})}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{-\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\|{h^{N_{1},L_{1}}_{kk_{1}^{\prime}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(k_{1}^{\prime}\to k)}\|{h^{N_{2},L_{2}}_{kk_{2}^{\prime}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(k_{2}^{\prime}\to k)}\|{h^{N_{3},L_{3}}_{kk_{3}^{\prime}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(kk_{3}^{\prime})}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{-\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{2}\big\|\langle\tau\rangle^{b}\widehat{h^{N_{j},L_{j}}_{k_{j}k_{j}^{\prime}}}(\tau)\big\|_{L_{\tau}^{2}(\ell^{2}_{k_{j}}\to\ell^{2}_{k_{j}^{\prime}})}\big\|\langle\tau\rangle^{b}\widehat{h^{N_{3},L_{3}}_{k_{3}k_{3}^{\prime}}}(\tau)\big\|_{L_{\tau}^{2}(\ell^{2}_{k_{3}}\ell^{2}_{k_{3}^{\prime}})}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{-\frac{3\alpha}{2}}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}+\gamma_{0}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{-\alpha},\end{split}

where we used the fact
b>\frac{1}{2}
and (
3.18
), which is sufficient for (
3.5
).

All the intermediate cases, from Case (b) to Case (g), can be controlled
similarly.

6.
Proof of Theorem
1.8

This section is devoted to the proof of Theorem
1.8
, which roughly means that the second Picard
iterate given in (
1.19
) converges in
H^{\frac{1}{2}+}

when
\alpha>1
, while fails to converge in
L^{2}
when
\alpha=1
.

We start with Theorem
1.8
(i), i.e. the case when
\alpha>1
.
The proof of this part actually follows from a similar argument as in Subsection
5.2.1
.
We give a proof here for completeness.
We rewrite

(Z_{N}^{(2)}(t))_{k}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\frac{g_{k_{1}}\overline{g_{k_{2}}}g_{k_{3}}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\cdot\Theta(t,\Phi),

where

{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}=\mathbf{1}_{\langle k\rangle<N}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\mathbf{1}_{N^{1-\delta}<k_{j}<N},

and

\Theta(t,\Phi)=\int^{t}_{0}e^{it^{\prime}\Phi(\bar{k})}dt^{\prime}.

Here
\Phi(\bar{k})=|k_{1}|^{\alpha}-|k_{2}|^{\alpha}+|k_{3}|^{\alpha}-|k|^{\alpha}
is given in (
1.23
).
We note that
\Theta(t,\Phi)
is bounded and also
\Theta(t,\Phi)=\frac{e^{it\Phi}-1}{i\Phi}
when
\Phi\neq 0
.
Therefore,

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\big[\|Z_{N}^{(2)}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2}\big]&\quad=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\frac{|\Theta(t,\Phi)|^{2}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}\\
&\quad\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\frac{\langle\Phi(\bar{k})\rangle^{-2}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}},\end{split}

(6.1)

provided
|t|\sim 1
.
By a similar argument as in (
4.2
), we obtain

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\big[\|Z_{N}^{(2)}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2}\big]&\quad\lesssim\sum_{m\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{\langle m\rangle^{2}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}\frac{{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\mathbf{1}_{\Phi=m+O(1)}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}\\
&\quad\lesssim\sup_{m\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}\frac{{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\mathbf{1}_{\Phi=m+O(1)}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]^{\alpha}}.\end{split}

Then we dyadically decompose
k_{1}
,
k_{2}
, and
k_{3}
to get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\big[\|Z_{N}^{(2)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\big]\lesssim\sup_{m\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}\frac{1}{N_{1}^{\alpha}N_{2}^{\alpha}N_{3}^{\alpha}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta,m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}},\end{split}

where

{\rm T}^{\delta,m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}={\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\mathbf{1}_{\Phi=m+O(1)}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\mathbf{1}_{\frac{N_{j}}{2}<\langle k_{j}\rangle\leq N_{j}}.

From Lemma
2.10
, we have

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta,m}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\leq|S|\lesssim N_{\max}N_{\min}.

Recall that
N_{1}
,
N_{2}
, and
N_{3}
are dyadic numbers bounded from below by
N^{1-\delta}
.
Then, we may continue with

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\big[\|Z_{N}^{(2)}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}^{2}\big]\lesssim\sum_{N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}\frac{N_{\max}N_{\min}}{N_{1}^{\alpha}N_{2}^{\alpha}N_{3}^{\alpha}}=\sum_{N^{1-\delta}<N_{\med}}N_{\med}^{-\alpha}\lesssim N^{-\alpha(1-\delta)}\lesssim N^{-1},\end{split}

provided
\alpha(1-\delta)>1
,
which finishes the proof of Theorem
1.8
(i).

Now we turn to Theorem
1.8
(ii), where
\alpha=1
. For this case,
we observe that if
k\geq 0
and
k_{i}\geq 0
, then
\Phi(\bar{k})=0
on
the hyperplane
k=k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}
. We also note that
|\Theta(t,0)|=|\int^{t}_{0}dt^{\prime}|=|t|\sim 1
. Then starting from the first line of
(
6.1
), the proof will be changed to the following

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}&\big[\|Z_{N}^{(2)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\big]\\
&=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\}\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\frac{|\Theta(t,\Phi)|^{2}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt][\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt][\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]}\\
&\geq\sum_{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\},\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\frac{|\Theta(t,0)|^{2}}{[\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{1}]\hskip-1.70717pt][\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{2}]\hskip-1.70717pt][\hskip-1.70717pt[k_{3}]\hskip-1.70717pt]}\\
&\gtrsim|\Theta(t,0)|^{2}\sum_{N^{1-\delta}<N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\leq N/4}(N_{1}N_{2}N_{3})^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k\\
k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\},\end{subarray}}{\rm T}^{\delta}_{kk_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}\bigg),\end{split}

(6.2)

where
N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}
are dyadic numbers. Let
S_{N}
be the set given by

S_{N}=\{(k,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})\in\mathbb{Z}^{4};k_{1}-k_{2}+k_{3}=k,k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\},0\leq k<N,k_{j}\in(N_{j}/2,N_{j})\},

such that
N^{1-\delta}<N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\leq N/4
. Then it is
easy to see that

|S_{N}|=|\{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})\in\mathbb{Z}^{3};k_{2}\not\in\{k_{1},k_{3}\},k_{j}\in(N_{j}/2,N_{j})\}|\gtrsim N_{1}N_{2}N_{3},

which together with (
6.2
) implies

\mathbb{E}\big[\|Z_{N}^{(2)}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T})}\big]\gtrsim\sum_{N^{1-\delta}<N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\leq N/4}1\gtrsim\delta^{3}(\log N)^{3}.

This finishes the proof of Theorem
1.8
(ii).
